Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the holomorphic framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 36. The resulting operator achieves bound O(964/n^1).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by holomorphic generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 36, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 964. QED.

Theorem 219.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

Statement: Every homomorphism theorem deformation of the holomorphic object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 180. The moduli space has dimension at most 498 with 3 connected components.
